Operational Semantics

We present the operational semantics of our session
\pi
-calculus for modelling the behaviour of affine timed processes,
including asynchronous communication, time progression, timeout activation, and failure handling.

Definition 2

(Semantics)
.

A
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{try-catch}}}}
context

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{E}}
is defined as
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{E}}\Coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{try}}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{E}}}\ \operatorname{\text{{catch}}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}\mathbin{\;\big|\;}[\,]
, and
a
reduction context

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{C}}
is defined as
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{C}}\Coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{C}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\mathbin{\;\big|\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}\right){{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{C}}}}\mathbin{\;\big|\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{def}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}D}}\;\mathbf{in}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{C}}}}\mathbin{\;\big|\;}[\,]
.

The reductions
\to
,
\rightarrowtail
, and
\rightharpoonup

are inductively defined in
Fig.

3
(top), with respect to a
structural congruence
\equiv
depicted in
Fig.

3
(bottom).
We write
\mathrel{\to{}^{\!\!\!*}}
,
\mathrel{\rightarrowtail{}^{\!\!\!*}}
, and
\mathrel{\rightharpoonup{}^{\!\!\!*}}

for their reflexive and transitive closures, respectively.

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}\!\not\!\!{\to}
(or

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}\!\not\!\!{\rightarrowtail}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}\!\not\!\!{\rightharpoonup}
)
means
\not\exists{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}

such that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\!\to\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
(or
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\!\rightarrowtail\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\!\rightharpoonup\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
)
is derivable. We say
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}

has a communication error
iff
\exists{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{C}}
with

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{C}}}\!\left[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\boldsymbol{\mathsf{cerr}}}}\right]
.

Remark 3
.

The
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{try-catch}}}}
contexts
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{E}}
in
Def.

2

are exclusively
used for defining reductions at the top level of processes,
without applying them for nested exception handling as in
[
18
]
.

We decompose the reduction rules in
Fig.

3

into three relations:

\rightarrowtail
represents
instantaneous
reductions
without time consumption,
\rightharpoonup
handles time-consuming steps,
and
\to
is a general relation that can arise either from

\rightarrowtail
by
[R-Ins]

or
\rightharpoonup
by
[R-TC]
. Now let us explain the operational semantics rules for our session
\pi
-calculus.

Communication
: Rules

[R-Out]
and
[R-In]
model asynchronous communication by queuing and dequeuing
pending
messages, respectively. Rule
[R-Err]
is triggered by a message label mismatch, resulting in a fatal

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\boldsymbol{\mathsf{c}}}
ommunication
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\boldsymbol{\mathsf{err}}}
or.

Time
:
Rule
[R-Det]
specifies a deterministic delay of a specific duration

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}
, where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}
is a solution to the clock constraint
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta}
.
Rule
[R-Time]
incorporates a time-passing function
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P})}
,
depicted in
Fig.

4
,
to represent time delays within a process.
This
partial
function simulates a delay of time
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}
that may occur at different parts of the process.
It is
undefined
only if
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
is a time-consuming delay, i.e.
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{delay}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta}})}{\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}}}
, or if the specified delay time
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}
exceeds the duration of a runtime deterministic delay, i.e.
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{delay}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t^{\prime}}})}{\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}}}
with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}>{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t^{\prime}}
. The latter case arises because deterministic delays must always adhere to their specified durations, e.g. if a program is instructed to sleep for 5 time units, it must strictly follow this duration.

Notably,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P})}
acts as the
only mechanism
for triggering
a timeout failure
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{timeout}}}[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}]
, resulting from a timed selection or branching.
Such a timeout failure occurs when
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P})}
is defined, and the specified delay
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}
exceeds a
deadline
set within
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
.

Cancellation
:
Rules
[C-In]
and
[C-Queue]
model the process cancellations.

[C-In]
is triggered only
when there are no messages in the queue that
can be received from
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}
via the endpoint

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}
.
Cancellation of a timed selection is expected to
eventually occur via
[C-Queue]
;
therefore,
there is no specific rule dedicated to it.
Similarly,
in our implementation,
the timed selection is not directly cancelled either.

Rules
[R-Can]
and
[C-Cat]
,
adapted from
[
32
]
,
state cancellations from other
parties.

[R-Can]

facilitates cancellation and generates a kill process, while
[C-Cat]
transitions
to the
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{catch}}}}
process
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}

due to the termination of session
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
,
where the
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{try}}}}
process
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
is communicating on
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
.
Therefore, the set of
subjects
of process
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
, denoted as
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}})}
, is included
in the side condition of
[C-Cat]
to ensure that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
has a prefix at
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
,
as defined below:

\footnotesize\begin{array}[]{c}\begin{array}[]{l}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\boldsymbol{\mathsf{cerr}}}})}=\emptyset\hskip 8.50012pt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}})}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}})}\hskip 8.50012pt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sigma}}}})}=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}^{\tiny{\mathscr{Q}}}\right\}}}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}\right){{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}})}\setminus(\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}_{i}}}]}\right\}}}_{i\in I}\cup\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}_{i}}}]}}^{\mathscr{Q}}\right\}}}_{i\in I})\\
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{def}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}(\widetilde{x})={{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}}\;\mathbf{in}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}})}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}})}\setminus\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{\widetilde{x}\right\}}}\text{ \,with\, }{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}\!\left\langle{\widetilde{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}}\right\rangle}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\nicefrac{{\widetilde{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}}}{{\widetilde{x}}}}\right\}}}})}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}^{\mathfrak{n}}[\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}]\mathbin{\!\oplus\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{m}}}}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}d}}\rangle\vphantom{x}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}^{\mathfrak{n}}[\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}]}\mathbin{\!\sum_{i\in I}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}_{\mathnormal{i}}}}}({x_{i}})\vphantom{x}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{i}}}}}{}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{cancel}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}})}{\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}})}=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\right\}}}\\[3.69885pt]
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{delay}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta}})}{\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{delay}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}})}{\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{try}}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}\ \operatorname{\text{{catch}}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{timeout}}}[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}]})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}})}\end{array}\end{array}

Subjects of processes determine sessions that may need cancellation, a crucial aspect for handling failed or
cancelled processes properly. In our definition, subjects not only denote the endpoints via which processes start interacting but also indicate whether they are used for message queue processes.
Specifically, an endpoint
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}
annotated with
\mathscr{Q}
signifies its use in a queue process.
This additional annotation, and thus the distinction it implies, is pivotal in formulating the typing rule for the
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{try-catch}}}}
process, as discussed later in
§​

4.4
, where we rely on subjects to exclude queue processes within any
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{try}}}}
construct.

Timeout Handling
: Rules
[R-Fail]
and
[R-FailCat]
address time failures.
In the event of a timeout, a killing process is generated.
Moreover, in
[R-FailCat]
,
the
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{catch}}}}
process
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}
is triggered.
To identify the session requiring termination,
the set of subjects of the failure process
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{timeout}}}[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}]
is considered in both rules as a side condition.
Note that a timeout arises exclusively from timed selection or branching. Therefore, the subject set of

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{timeout}}}[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}]
must contain a
single
endpoint devoid of
\mathscr{Q}
, indicating the generation of only one killing process.

Standard
: Rules
[R-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}
]
,
[R-Ctx]
, and
[R-
\equiv
]

are standard
[
43
,
32
]
.
[R-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}
]
expands process definitions when invoked;

[R-Ctx]
and
[R-
\equiv
]

allow processes to reduce under reduction contexts
and through structural congruence,
respectively.
Rule
[R-
\equiv
T]

introduces a timed variant of
[R-
\equiv
]
, enabling
time-consuming reductions via structural congruence.

Congruence
: As shown in
Fig.

3
(bottom), we introduce additional congruence rules related to queues, delays, and process killings, alongside standard rules from
[
43
]
. Specifically, two rules are proposed for queues: the first addresses the
garbage
collection of queues that are not referenced by any process, while the second rearranges messages with different receivers.
The rule for delays states that adding a delay of zero time units has no effect on the process execution.
The rule regarding process killings eliminates duplicate kills.

Example 4
.

Consider the processes:
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{1}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}]}}^{0.4}[\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}}}]\mathbin{\!\oplus\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{Data}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}}}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{2}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}]}}^{0.3}[\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}}}]{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{Data}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}}}}
, and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{3}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}
.
Rule
[C-Cat]
can be applied to
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{try}}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{1}}}\ \operatorname{\text{{catch}}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}\lightning
,
as
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{1}}})}=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}]}\right\}}}
satisfies its side condition.
However, neither
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{timeout}}}[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{1}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{2}}}]
nor
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{timeout}}}[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{3}}}]
can generate the killing process

{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}\lightning
, as

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{1}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{2}}})}=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}]},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}]}\right\}}}
,
whereas

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{3}}})}=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}]}}^{\mathscr{Q}}\right\}}}
.

Example 5
.

Processes
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}}
, and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}}

interact on a session
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
:

\footnotesize\begin{array}[]{l}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{delay}}}({C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}=6.5})}{\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\,\,{\footnotesize\text{ where }}\,\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{try}}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}]}}^{0.3}[\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}}}]\mathbin{\!\oplus\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{Data}}}}}}}\ \operatorname{\text{{catch}}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{cancel}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}]}})}{}}}\\[1.42262pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{delay}}}({C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}=6})}{\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\,\,{\footnotesize\text{ where }}\,\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}]}}^{0.2}[\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}}}]}\mathbin{\!\sum\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{\left\{\begin{array}[]{@{\hskip 0mm}l@{\hskip 0mm}}.0pt\lx@intercol{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{Data}}\vphantom{x}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}]}}^{0.3}[\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}}}]\mathbin{\!\oplus\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{Data}}}}}}}}\hfil\hskip 0.0pt\\
.0pt\lx@intercol{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{fail}}\vphantom{x}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}]}}^{0.4}[\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}}}]\mathbin{\!\oplus\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{fatal}}}}}}}}\hfil\hskip 0.0pt\end{array}\right\}}}}}\\[1.42262pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{delay}}}({C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}=6})}{\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\,\,{\footnotesize\text{ where }}\,\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}]}}^{0.8}[\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}}}]}\mathbin{\!\sum\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{Data}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{fatal}}}\right\}}}}}\end{array}

Process
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}}
delays for exactly 6.5 time units before executing process

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}}
. Here,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}}
attempts to use
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}]}

to send
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{Data}}
to
\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}
within 0.3 time units. If the attempt fails, the cancellation of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}]}
is triggered.
Process
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}}
waits for precisely 6 time units before using

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}]}
to receive either
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{Data}}
or
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{fail}}
from

\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}
within 0.2 time units; subsequently, in the first case, it uses

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}]}
to send
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{Data}}
to
\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}
within 0.3 time units, while in the latter, it uses
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}]}
to send
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{fail}}
to
\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}
within 0.4 time units. Similarly, process
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}}
waits 6 time units before using
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}]}
to receive either

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{Data}}
or
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{fatal}}
from
\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}
within 0.8 time units.

In
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}]}
can only start sending
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{Data}}
to

\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}
after 6.5 time units, whereas in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}]}
must receive the message from
\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}
within 0.2 time units after a 6-time unit delay. Consequently,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}]}
fails to receive the message from
\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}
within the specified interval, resulting in a timeout failure, i.e.

\footnotesize\begin{array}[]{l@{\hskip.5mm}l@{\hskip.5mm}l}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}}\hfil\hskip 1.42262pt&\rightarrowtail\hfil\hskip 1.42262pt&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{delay}}}({6.5})}{\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{delay}}}({6})}{\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{delay}}}({6})}{\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\\[1.42262pt]
\hfil\hskip 1.42262pt&\rightharpoonup\hfil\hskip 1.42262pt&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{6.5}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{delay}}}({6.5})}{\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{delay}}}({6})}{\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{delay}}}({6})}{\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}})}\\[1.42262pt]
\hfil\hskip 1.42262pt&\equiv\hfil\hskip 1.42262pt&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{timeout}}}[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}}}]\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{0.5}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}})}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\end{array}

Therefore, the kill process
{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}\lightning
is generated from
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{timeout}}}[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}}}]
, successfully terminating the process
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}}
by the following reductions:

\footnotesize\begin{array}[]{r@{\hskip 2mm}l@{\hskip 2mm}}\hskip 5.69054pt&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{timeout}}}[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}}}]\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{0.5}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}})}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\hfil\hskip 5.69054pt\\[1.42262pt]
\rightarrowtail\hskip 5.69054pt&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}\lightning\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{0.5}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}})}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\hfil\hskip 5.69054pt\\[1.42262pt]
\rightarrowtail\hskip 5.69054pt&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{cancel}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}]}})}{}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}\lightning\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\hfil\hskip 5.69054pt\\[1.42262pt]
\rightarrowtail\hskip 5.69054pt&{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}\lightning\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}\lightning\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\;\equiv\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}\lightning\hfil\hskip 5.69054pt\end{array}

4
Affine Timed Multiparty Session Type System

In this section, we introduce our affine timed multiparty session type system.
We begin by exploring the types used in
ATMP
, as well as subtyping and projection, in
§​

4.1
. We furnish a Labelled Transition System (LTS) semantics for typing environments (collections of timed local types and queue types) in
§​

4.2
, and timed global types in
§​

4.3
,
illustrating their relationship with
Thms.

14
and
15
.
Furthermore, we present a type system for our
ATMP
session
\pi
-calculus in
§​

4.4
.
Finally, we show the main properties of the type system:

subject reduction
(
Thm.

21
),
session fidelity
(
Thm.

25
), and
deadlock-freedom
(
Thm.

28
),
in
§​

4.5
.

4.1
Timed Multiparty Session Types

\small\begin{array}[]{r@{\quad}c@{\quad}l@{\quad}l}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}\hskip 9.24994pt&\Coloneqq\hfil\hskip 9.24994pt&({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})\hfil\hskip 9.24994pt&\text{\footnotesize{Session Type}}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}\hskip 9.24994pt&\Coloneqq\hfil\hskip 9.24994pt&{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}})\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{O}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{O}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{I}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{I}}}_{i}}\}\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}\hfil\hskip 9.24994pt&{\footnotesize\text{Transmission}}\\

\hskip 9.24994pt&\mathbin{\;\big|\;}\hfil\hskip 9.24994pt&{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}}\hskip 9.24994pt\mathbin{\;\big|\;}\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}\hskip 9.24994pt\mathbin{\;\big|\;}\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{end}}\hfil\hskip 9.24994pt&\text{\footnotesize Recursion, Type variable, Termination}\\[1.93748pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\hskip 9.24994pt&\Coloneqq\hfil\hskip 9.24994pt&\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}})}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda_{i}}\}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}\hfil\hskip 9.24994pt&\text{\footnotesize External choice}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt&\mathbin{\;\big|\;}\hfil\hskip 9.24994pt&\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}})}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda_{i}}\}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}\hfil\hskip 9.24994pt&\text{\footnotesize Internal choice}\\
\hskip 9.24994pt&\mathbin{\;\big|\;}\hfil\hskip 9.24994pt&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}}\ \mathbin{\;\big|\;}\ {\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}\ \mathbin{\;\big|\;}\ {\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}\hfil\hskip 9.24994pt&\text{\footnotesize Recursion, Type variable, Termination}\\
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}\hskip 9.24994pt&\Coloneqq\hfil\hskip 9.24994pt&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mathbin{\!\mathbf{!}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}}}\mathbin{\;\big|\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}\hfil\hskip 9.24994pt&\text{\footnotesize{Queue types}}\end{array}

Figure 5
:
Syntax of timed global types, timed local types, and queue types.

Affine session frameworks keep the original system’s type-level syntax intact, requiring no changes.
To introduce affine timed asynchronous multiparty session types,
we simply need to augment global and local types with clock constraints and resets
introduced in
§​

3

to derive
timed global and local types
.
The syntax of types used in this paper is presented in
Fig.

5
. As usual, all types are required to be closed and have guarded recursion variables.

Sorts

are ranged over
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}},\ldots
, and
facilitate the delegation of the
remaining behaviour
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}
to the receiver, who can execute it under any
clock assignment satisfying
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta}
.

Timed Global Types

are ranged over
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G},{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}},{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}},\ldots
, and describe an
overview
of the behaviour for all roles (
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{s}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{t}}}},\ldots
) belonging to a (fixed) set
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathcal{R}}
. The set of roles in a timed global type

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
is denoted as
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}
, while the set of its free variables as
\operatorname{fv}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\right)
. We explain each syntactic construct of global types as follows.

Transmission

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}})\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{O}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{O}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{I}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{I}}}_{i}}\}\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}

represents a message sent from role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}
to role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}
, with labels
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}
,
payload types
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}
(which are sorts),
and continuations
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}
, where
i
is taken from an index set
I
, and

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}
taken from a fixed set of all labels
\mathcal{M}
.
Each branch
is associated with a
time assertion

consisting of four components:
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{O}}}_{i}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{O}}}_{i}}
for the output (sending) action,
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{I}}}_{i}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{I}}}_{i}}
for the input (receiving) action.
These components specify the clock constraint and reset predicate for the respective actions.
A message can be sent (or received) at any time satisfying the guard
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{O}}}_{i}}
(or
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{I}}}_{i}}
),
and the clocks in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{O}}}_{i}}
(or
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{I}}}_{i}}
) are reset upon sending (or receiving).

In addition to the standard requirements for global types, including the index set being non-empty (
I\neq\emptyset
), labels
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}
being pairwise distinct, and self-receptions being excluded (
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}
),
we impose a condition from
[
6
]
, stating that sets of clocks “owned” by
different roles, i.e. those that can be read and reset, must be pairwise disjoint. Furthermore,
the clock constraint and reset predicate of an output or input action performed by a role
are defined only over the clocks owned by that role. For example,

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}{\to}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{Data}}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}6\leq C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}\leq 7,C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}:=0,7\leq C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}\leq 8,C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}:=0}\}\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{end}}}}}\right\}}}
violates this assumption.
Both
\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}
’s sending and
\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}
’s receiving actions have time constraints defined over
C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}
,
which can be owned by either of them. The same issue exists for the reset
\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}\right\}}}
.

Transmission en route

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}})\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{O}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{O}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{I}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{I}}}_{i}}\}\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}~(j\in I)
is a
runtime
construct
to represent a message
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{j}}
sent by
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}
, and yet to be received by
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}
.
The distinction between
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\rightarrow}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\rightsquigarrow}
is crucial for simulating asynchronous communication.

Recursion

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}}
and
termination

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{end}}
(omitted where unambiguous)
are standard
[
13
]
. Note that contractive requirements
[
38
, §21.8]
, i.e. ensuring that each recursion variable
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}
is bound within a
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{\ldots}}
and is guarded, are applied in recursive types.

Timed Local Types (timed session types)

are ranged over
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}U},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}U^{\prime}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}U_{i}},\ldots
, and describe the behaviour of a
single
role.
An
internal choice
(
selection
)

\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}})}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda_{i}}\}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}

(or
external choice
(
branching
)

\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}})}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda_{i}}\}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}
)
states that the
current
role is to
send
to (or

receive
from) the role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}
when
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta_{i}}
is satisfied,
followed by resetting the clocks in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda_{i}}
.

Recursive
and
termination
types are defined
similarly to timed global types.
The requirements for the index set, labels,
clock constraints, and reset predicates
in timed local types mirror those in timed global types.

Queue Types

are ranged over
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}_{i}},\ldots
, and represent (possibly empty) sequences of
message types

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mathbin{\!\mathbf{!}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}})}
having receiver
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}
, label
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}
, and payload type
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}
(omitted when
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}\!=\!({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}})
). As interactions in our formalisation are asynchronous,
queue types are used to capture the states in which messages are in transit.
We adopt the notation
\operatorname{receivers}(\cdot)
from
§​

3
to denote
the set of
receivers
in

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}
as
\operatorname{receivers}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}})
as well, with a similar definition.

Subtyping

We introduce a subtyping relation
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}
on timed local types
in
Def.

6
, based on
the standard behaviour-preserving subtyping
[
43
]
.
This relation indicates that a smaller type entails fewer external choices but more internal choices.

Definition 6

(Subtyping)
.

The
subtyping relation

\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}
is coinductively defined:

\small\begin{array}[]{c}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}})}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda_{i}}\}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I\cup J}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}_{i}}})}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta^{\prime}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda^{\prime}_{i}}\}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}\lx@proof@logical@and\forall i\in I{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}_{i}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta_{i}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta^{\prime}_{i}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda_{i}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda^{\prime}_{i}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}\\[1.42262pt]
\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}})}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda_{i}}\}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}_{i}}})}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta^{\prime}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda^{\prime}_{i}}\}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I\cup J}}\lx@proof@logical@and\forall i\in I{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}_{i}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta_{i}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta^{\prime}_{i}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda_{i}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda^{\prime}_{i}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}\\[1.42262pt]
({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}{}\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}}}\right\}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}{}\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}}}\right\}}}\end{array}

Projection

of a timed global type
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
onto a role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}
yields a timed local type.
Our definition of projection in
Def.

7
is mostly standard
[
43
]
,
with the addition of projecting time assertions onto the sender and receiver, respectively.
For example, when projecting a transmission from
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}
to
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}
onto
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}
(or
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}
),
an internal (or external) choice with time assertions for output (or input) is obtained, provided that
each branching’s continuation is projectable.
For the projection of the transmission onto other participants
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}
, a
merge operator
is used to ensure the “compatibility” of the projections of all continuations.

Definition 7

(Projection)
.

The
projection of a timed global type
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
onto a role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}
,
written as
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\upharpoonright}\,\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}
, is:

\small\begin{array}[]{c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\left({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}})\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{O}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{O}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{I}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{I}}}_{i}}\}\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}\right)}{\upharpoonright}\,\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}=\!\left\{\begin{array}[]{@{}l@{\hskip 2mm}l@{}}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}})}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{O}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{O}}}_{i}}\}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}}{\upharpoonright}\,\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}})\right\}_{i\in I}}\hfil\hskip 5.69054pt&\text{\footnotesize if\, ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}$}\\[2.84526pt]
\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}})}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{I}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{I}}}_{i}}\}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}}{\upharpoonright}\,\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}})\right\}_{i\in I}}\hfil\hskip 5.69054pt&\text{\footnotesize if\, ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}$}\\[2.84526pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\bigsqcap_{i\in I}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}}{\upharpoonright}\,\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}}}\hfil\hskip 5.69054pt&\text{\footnotesize otherwise
}\end{array}\right.\\[22.76219pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\left({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}})\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{O}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{O}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{I}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{I}}}_{i}}\}\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}\right)}{\upharpoonright}\,\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}=\!\left\{\begin{array}[]{@{}l@{\hskip 2mm}l@{}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{j}}}{\upharpoonright}\,\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}\hfil\hskip 5.69054pt&\text{\footnotesize if\, ${\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}$}\\[2.84526pt]

Example 8
.

Take the timed global type
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
, and timed local types
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}}
from
§​

2.1
.
Consider a timed global type
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{\text{data}}}
,
derived from remote data (
Fig.

1(b)
) as well,
representing data transmission from

\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}
to
\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}
via
\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}
:

\footnotesize{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{\text{data}}}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}{\to}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{Data}}}}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}6\leq C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}\leq 7,C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}:=0,6\leq C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}\leq 7,\emptyset}\}\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}}}\right\}}}

which can be projected onto roles
\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}
,

\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}
, and
\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}
, respectively, as:

\footnotesize\begin{array}[]{c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{\text{data}}}}{\upharpoonright}\,\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}}}}=\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{Data}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}6\leq C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}\leq 7,C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}:=0}\}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}\hskip 17.00024pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{\text{data}}}}{\upharpoonright}\,\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\upharpoonright}\,\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}}\\[1.42262pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{\text{data}}}}{\upharpoonright}\,\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}}}}=\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{Data}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}6\leq C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}\leq 7,\emptyset}\}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\upharpoonright}\,\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}}}}=\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{Data}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}6\leq C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}\leq 7,\emptyset}\}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}}\end{array}

4.2
Typing Environments

To reflect the behaviour of timed global types (
§​

4.3
),
present a typing system for our session
\pi
-calculus (
§​

4.4
),
and introduce type-level properties (
§​

4.5
),
we formalise
typing environments
in
Def.

9
, followed by their Labelled Transition System (LTS) semantics in
Def.

10
.

\small\begin{array}[]{c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\equiv}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}\vphantom{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\equiv}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}}\hskip 18.49988pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mathbin{\!\mathbf{!}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{1}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{1}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mathbin{\!\mathbf{!}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{2}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{2}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}}}}}\;\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\equiv}}\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mathbin{\!\mathbf{!}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{2}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{2}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mathbin{\!\mathbf{!}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{1}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{1}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}}}}}\mathchoice{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\textstyle{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\scriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}$}}\\[1.42262pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\equiv}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}\hskip 18.49988pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mathbin{\!\mathbf{!}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}}}}}\;\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\equiv}}\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\mathbin{\!\mathbf{!}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}}}}}\hskip 18.49988pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\equiv}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}})}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\equiv}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}}\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\equiv}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}})}\\[2.84526pt]
\hline\cr\\[-7.11317pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}\vphantom{X}\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mathbin{\!\mathbf{!}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mathbin{\!\mathbf{!}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}}}}\lx@proof@logical@and\mathchoice{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\textstyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\scriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}$}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}}\\[1.42262pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}})}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}\hskip 18.49988pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}})}}}\lx@proof@logical@and{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}})}\end{array}

Figure 6
:
Congruence (top) and subtyping (bottom) rules for timed-session/queue types.

Definition 9

(Typing Environments)
.

The typing environments
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
are defined as:

\small{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}\;\;\Coloneqq\;\;{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\emptyset}\mathbin{\;\big|\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}{:}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{1}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{1}}})},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{n}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{n}}})}}}\hskip 9.24994pt\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\,\Coloneqq\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\emptyset}\mathbin{\;\big|\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}}}\mathbin{\;\big|\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}}}

where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}
is a
timed-session/queue
type:
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}\Coloneqq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}\mathbin{\;\big|\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}\mathbin{\;\big|\;}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}}}
, i.e. either a timed session type, a queue type, or a combination.

The environment
composition

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{1}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{2}}

is defined iff

\forall{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{1}}}\right)}\cap{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{2}}}\right)}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{i}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}\!=\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}\text{ and }{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\!j}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}\!=\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}\text{ with $i,j\in\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{1,2\right\}}}$}
, and for all such
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}
, we posit
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{1}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{2}})\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}}}
.

We write
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\right)}=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}\right\}}}
iff for any
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\right)}
, there
is
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}
(i.e.
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
only contains session
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
).
We write
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}\not\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
iff

\forall{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}\not\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\right)}
(i.e. session
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
does not occur in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
).
We write
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}
iff
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}}\right)}=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}\right\}}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}}\right)}\!\subseteq\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\right)}
,
and
\forall{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}\!\in\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\right)}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}
(i.e. restriction of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
to session
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
).
We denote updates as
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}]}
:

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}]}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\tau}]}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c^{\prime}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c^{\prime}}}\right)}
(where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c^{\prime}}
).

Congruence and subtyping are imposed on typing environments:

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\equiv}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
(resp.
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
)
iff
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}\right)}
and
\forall{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\right)}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\equiv}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}
(resp.
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}
), incorporating additional
congruence and subtyping rules for time-session/queue types, as depicted in
Fig.

6
.

In
Def.

9
, the typing environment
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}
maps process variables
to
n
-tuples of timed session types, while

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
maps variables to timed session types, and
channels with roles to timed-session/queue types.
Note that in our typing environments, timed session types are annotated with clock valuations,
denoted as
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}
.
This enables us to capture timing information within the type system, facilitating the tracking of the (virtual) time at which the next action can be validated during the execution of a process.

The congruence relation
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\equiv}}
for timed-session/queue types is inductively defined as in
Fig.

6
(top), reordering queued messages with different receivers. Subtyping for timed-session/queue types
extends
Def.

6
with rules in
Fig.

6
(bottom): particularly,
rule
[Sub-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}
]
states that a sequence of queued message types is a subtype of another if messages in the same position have identical receivers and labels, and their payload sorts are related by subtyping.

\small\begin{array}[]{c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}}})}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}}}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{t}}}}}}\right\}}}})}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}}}\lx@proof@logical@and{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\mathchoice{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\textstyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\scriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}$}}\hskip
