Typing environment semantics.

Definition 10

(Typing Environment Reduction)
.

Let
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}
be a transition label of the form
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}!}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}
, or
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}
. The
typing environment transition
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}
is inductively defined by the rules in

Fig.

7
. We write
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}
iff

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
for some
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
.
We define two
reductions

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}_{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
(where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
is a session) and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
as follows:

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}_{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
holds iff

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}

with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}\in\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}!}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}\,\middle|\,{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}},{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathcal{R}}}\right\}}}
(where

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathcal{R}}
is the set of all roles).
We write
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}_{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}
iff
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}_{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
for some
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
, and
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to^{\!*}}_{\!\!\!{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}}
as the reflexive and transitive closure of
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}_{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}
;

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
holds iff
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}_{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
for some
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
.
We write
{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\!\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}}
iff
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}}\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
for some
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
, and
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}}{}^{\!\!\!*}}}
as the reflexive and transitive closure of
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}}
.

The label
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}!}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}
indicates that
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}

sends the message
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}
to
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}
on session
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
,
while
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}
denotes
the reception of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}
from
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}
by
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}
on
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
.
Additionally, the label
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}~(\in\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0})
represents a time action modelling the passage of time.

The (
highlighted
) main modifications in the reduction rules for typing environments,
compared to standard rules, concern time.
Rule
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-
\oplus
]
states that an entry can perform an
output transition by appending a message at the respective queue within the time specified by the output clock constraint.
Dually,
rule
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-
\&
]
allows an entry to execute an input transition, consuming a message from the corresponding queue within the specified input clock constraint, provided that the payloads are compatible through subtyping.
Note that in both rules, the associated clock valuation of the reduced
entry must be updated according to the reset.

Rules
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-,x]
and
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-,
\tau
]
pertain to
untimed
reductions,
i.e.
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}
, within a larger environment.
Rule
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-Ts]
models time passing on an entry of timed session type by incrementing the associated clock valuation, while rule
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-Tq]
specifies that an entry of queue type is not affected with respect to time progression.
Thus,
rule
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-Tc]
captures the corresponding time behaviour for a timed-session/queue type entry.
Additionally, rule
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-,T]
ensures that time elapses
uniformly across compatibly composed environments.
Other rules are standard:
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-
\mu
]
is for recursion,
and
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-struct]

ensures that reductions are closed under congruence.

The reduction
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}_{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
indicates
that the typing environment
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
can advance on session
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
, involving any roles, while

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\to}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
signifies
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
progressing on any session.
This distinction helps in illustrating properties of typed processes discussed in
§​

4.5
.

4.3
Relating Timed Global Types and Typing Environments

One of our main results is establishing an operational relationship between the semantics of timed global types and typing environments, ensuring the
correctness
of processes typed by environments that reflect timed global types.
To accomplish this, we begin by assigning LTS semantics to timed global types.

Semantics of Timed Global Types

Similar to that of typing environments,
we define the LTS semantics for timed global types
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}

over tuples of the form

\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle
,
where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}
is a clock valuation.
Additionally, we specify the subject of an action
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}

as its responsible principal:

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subject}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}!}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subject}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}})}=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}\right\}}}
, and

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subject}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}})}=\emptyset
.

I\mathchoice{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}\models{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{I}}}_{j}}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\textstyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}\models{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{I}}}_{j}}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\scriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}\models{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{I}}}_{j}}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}\models{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{I}}}_{j}}$}}\mathchoice{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{I}}}_{j}}\mapsto 0]$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\textstyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{I}}}_{j}}\mapsto 0]$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\scriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{I}}}_{j}}\mapsto 0]$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{I}}}_{j}}\mapsto 0]$}}\\[3.87498pt]
\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}})\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{A}_{i}}\}\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}})\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{A}_{i}}\}\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}}\rangle\lx@proof@logical@and\forall i\in \langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}})\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{A}_{i}}\}\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}})\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{A}_{i}}\}\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}}\rangle\lx@proof@logical@and\forall i\in I:\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}_{i}}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}_{i}}}\rangle{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}\notin{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subject}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}})}\mathchoice{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\textstyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\scriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}$}}{\hbox{\pagecolor{yellow!40}$\scriptscriptstyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}$}}\par\end{array}

Figure 8
:
Timed global type reduction rules, where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathcal{A}_{i}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{O}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{O}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{I}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{I}}}_{i}}
.

Definition 11

(Timed Global Type Reduction)
.

The
timed global type
transition

\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}

is inductively
defined by the rules in
Fig.

8
.
We denote

\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\xrightarrow{\phantom{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle

if there
exists
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}
such that

\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle
,

\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\xrightarrow{\phantom{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}
if there exists

\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle
such that

\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\xrightarrow{\phantom{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle
, and
\xrightarrow{}{}^{\!\!\!*}
as the transitive and reflexive closure of
\xrightarrow{\phantom{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}
.

In
Fig.

8
, the (
highlighted
) changes from the standard
global type reduction rules
[
13
]
focus on time.
Rule
[GR-t]
accounts for the passage of time by incrementing the clock valuation.
Rules
[GR-
\oplus
]
and
[GR-
\&
]

model the sending and receiving of messages within specified clock constraints, respectively.
Both rules also require the adjustment of the clock valuation using the reset predicate.
Rule
[GR-
\mu
]
handles recursion.
Finally, rules
[GR-Ctx-i]
and
[GR-Ctx-ii]

allow reductions of (intermediate) global types causally independent of their prefixes.
Note that the execution of any timed global type transition always starts with an initial clock valuation
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}^{0}
,
i.e. all clocks in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}
are set to
0
.

Associating Timed Global Types with Typing Environments

We are now ready to establish a
new
relationship,
association
, between timed global types and typing environments. This association, which is more general than projection (
Def.

7
) by incorporating
subtyping
\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}
(
Def.

6
), plays a crucial role in formulating the typing rules (
§​

4.4
) and demonstrating the properties of typed processes (
§​

4.5
).

Definition 12

(Association)
.

A typing environment
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
is
associated with
a timed global type
\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle
for
a multiparty session
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
,
written

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}}
,
iff

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}

can be split into three (possibly empty) sub-environments

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\Delta}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}}}
where:

3.

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}}
is associated with
\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle
for
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
, provided as:

(i)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}}}\right)}=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\,\middle|\,{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}}\right\}}}
;

(ii)

\forall{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}}}\right)}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}})}
;

(iii)

\forall{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}{\upharpoonright}\,\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}
; and

(iv)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}=\sqcup_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}
(recall that
\sqcup
is an overriding union).

4.

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\Delta}}
is associated with
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
for
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
, given as follows:

(i)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\Delta}}}\right)}=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\,\middle|\,{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}\in{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{roles}({\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}})}}\right\}}}
;

(ii)

\forall{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\Delta}}}\right)}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\Delta}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}
;

(iii)

if
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{end}}
or
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mu{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{t}}}}.{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}}
, then

\forall{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\Delta}}}\right)}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\Delta}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}
;

(iv)

if
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{:}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}})\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{O}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{O}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{I}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{I}}}_{i}}\}\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}
, then

(a1)

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}\notin\operatorname{receivers}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\Delta}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)})
, and

(a2)

\forall i\in I
:
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\Delta}}
is associated with
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}
for
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
;

(v)

if
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}})\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{O}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{O}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{I}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{I}}}_{i}}\}\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}
, then

(b1)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\Delta}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mathbin{\!\mathbf{!}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{j}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}_{j}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}}}
with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}_{j}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{j}}
, and

(b2)

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\Delta}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}]}
is associated with
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{j}}
for
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
.

5.

\forall{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}}}}\right)}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}})}}}
.

The association
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\cdot}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\cdot}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\cdot}}}
is a
binary relation over timed global types
\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle
and typing environments
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
,
parameterised by multiparty sessions
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
.
There are three requirements for the association:

(1)

the typing environment
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
must include two entries for each role of the global type
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}

in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
: one of timed session type and another of queue type;

(2)

the timed session type entries in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
reflect
\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle
by ensuring
that:

(a)

they align with the projections of
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
via subtyping, and

(b)

their clock valuations match
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}
;

(3)

the queue type entries in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
correspond to the transmissions en route in
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}
.

Note that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}}}
is specifically used to associate
typing environments and
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{end}}
-types
\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{end}}}\rangle
, as in this case, both
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\Delta}}
are empty.

Example 13
.

Consider
the timed global type
\langle{\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}=0,C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}=0,C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}=0\right\}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{\text{data}}}}\rangle
, where
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{\text{data}}}
is from
Ex.

8
, and a typing environment

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\text{data}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\text{data}}}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\Delta_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\text{data}}}}}
, where:

\footnotesize\begin{array}[]{l@{\hskip 0mm}l@{\hskip.6mm}l}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\text{data}}}}}\hfil\hskip 0.0pt&=\hfil\hskip 1.70717pt&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}]}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}=0\right\}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{Data}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}6\leq C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}\leq 7,C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}:=0}\}}})}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}\\[1.42262pt]
\hfil\hskip 0.0pt&\hfil\hskip 1.70717pt&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}]}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}=0\right\}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{\begin{array}[]{@{\hskip 0mm}l@{\hskip 0mm}}.0pt\lx@intercol{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{Data}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}6\leq C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}\leq 7,\emptyset}\}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{Data}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}6\leq C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}\leq 7,C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}:=0}\}}}}\hfil\hskip 0.0pt\\
.0pt\lx@intercol{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{fail}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}6\leq C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}\leq 7,\emptyset}\}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{fatal}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}6\leq C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}\leq 7,C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}:=0}\}}}}\hfil\hskip 0.0pt\end{array}\right\}}})}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}\\[1.42262pt]
\hfil\hskip 0.0pt&\hfil\hskip 1.70717pt&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}]}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}=0\right\}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{\begin{array}[]{@{\hskip 0mm}l@{\hskip 0mm}}.0pt\lx@intercol{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{Data}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}6\leq C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}\leq 7,C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}:=0}\}}\hfil\hskip 0.0pt\\
.0pt\lx@intercol{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{fatal}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}6\leq C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}\leq 7,C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}:=0}\}}\hfil\hskip 0.0pt\end{array}\right\}}})}}}\\[1.42262pt]
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\Delta_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\text{data}}}}}\hfil\hskip 0.0pt&=\hfil\hskip 1.70717pt&{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}]}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}]}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}]}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}}}\end{array}

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\text{data}}}
is associated with
\langle{\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}=0,C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}=0,C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}=0\right\}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{\text{data}}}}\rangle
for
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
, which can be formally verified by ensuring that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\text{data}}}
satisfies all conditions outlined in
Def.

12
.
Intuitively,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\text{data}}}}}
is associated with
\langle{\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}}=0,C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}}=0,C_{\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}}=0\right\}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{\text{data}}}}\rangle
because:

(1)

\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sen}}}
sends
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{Data}}
, while
\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Sat}}}
and

\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{Ser}}}
expect to receive additional messages
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{fail}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{fatal}}
, respectively. Consequently, the entries in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\text{data}}}}}
adhere to the expected communication behaviour of each role in
of
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{\text{data}}}
,
though with more
input messages;

(2)

the time tracking for each entry in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\text{data}}}}}

matches the clock valuation of
{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{\text{data}}}
.

Additionally,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\Delta_{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\text{data}}}}}
only contains empty queues, aligning with that

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{\text{data}}}
is not currently in transit.

We establish the operational correspondence between a timed global type and
its associated typing environment, our main result for timed multiparty session types, through two theorems:
Thm.

14

demonstrates that every possible reduction of a typing environment is mirrored by a corresponding action in reductions of the associated timed global type, while
Thm.

15
indicates that the reducibility of a timed global type is equivalent to its associated
environment.
The proofs of
Thms.

14
and
15
are by inductions on reductions of typing environments and timed global types, respectively, with further details in
§​

A.5
.

Theorem 14

(Completeness of Association)
.

Given associated timed global type
\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle
and typing environment
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
:

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}}
.
If

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
, then there exists
\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle

such that

\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}}
.

Theorem 15

(Soundness of Association)
.

Given associated timed global type
\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle

and typing environment
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
:

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}}
.
If

\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\xrightarrow{\phantom{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}
,
then there exists
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha^{\prime}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime}}}
,

\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime\prime}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}}\rangle
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
, and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
, such that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}}
,

\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha^{\prime}}}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime\prime}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}}\rangle
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha^{\prime}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
, and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime\prime}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime\prime}}}\rangle}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}}}
.

Remark 16
.

We formulate a soundness theorem that does not mirror the completeness theorem, differing from
prior work such as
[
13
]
.
This choice stems from our reliance on subtyping (
Def.

6
),
notably
[Sub-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}
]
. In our framework, a timed local type in the typing environment might offer fewer selection branches
compared to the corresponding projected timed local type. Consequently, certain sending actions with their associated clock valuations may remain uninhabited within the timed global type. Consider, e.g. a timed global type:

\footnotesize\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{\text{r}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{\text{r}}}}\rangle=\langle{\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{C_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}=3,C_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}=3\right\}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{\to}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{:}\left\{{\begin{array}[]{@{\hskip 0mm}l@{\hskip 0mm}}.0pt\lx@intercol{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{1}}}}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}0\leq C_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\leq 1,\emptyset,1\leq C_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\leq 2,\emptyset}\}\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{end}}}}\hfil\hskip 0.0pt\\
.0pt\lx@intercol{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{2}}}}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}2\leq C_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\leq 4,\emptyset,5\leq C_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\leq 6,\emptyset}\}\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathbf{end}}}}\hfil\hskip 0.0pt\end{array}}\right\}}}\rangle

An associated typing environment
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\text{r}}}
may have:

\footnotesize{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\text{r}}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{C_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}=3\right\}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{1}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}0\leq C_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\leq 1,\emptyset}\}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}})}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\geqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{C_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}=3\right\}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus}\left\{\begin{array}[]{@{\hskip 0mm}l@{\hskip 0mm}}.0pt\lx@intercol{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{1}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}0\leq C_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\leq 1,\emptyset}\}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}\hfil\hskip 0.0pt\\
.0pt\lx@intercol{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{2}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}2\leq C_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}\leq 4,\emptyset}\}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}\hfil\hskip 0.0pt\end{array}\right\}})}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}}}

While the timed global type
\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{\text{r}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{\text{r}}}}\rangle
might transition through

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}:}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}!}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}:}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{2}}
,
the associated environment
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\text{r}}}
cannot.
Nevertheless, our soundness theorem
adequately
guarantees communication safety (communication matches) via association.

4.4
Affine Timed Multiparty Session Typing System

We now present a typing system for
ATMP
, which relies on

typing judgments

of the form:

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}\quad\text{(with ${\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}$ omitted when empty)}

This judgement indicates that the process
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
adheres to the usage of its variables and channels
as specified in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
(
Def.

9
), guided by the process types in
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}
(
Def.

9
).
Our typing system is defined inductively by the typing rules shown in
Fig.

9
,
with channels annotated for convenience, especially those bound by process definitions and restrictions.

\small\begin{array}[]{c}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}\mathbin{\!:\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{1}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{1}}})},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{n}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{n}}})}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}\right)={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{1}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{1}}})},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{n}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{n}}})}}\hskip 18.49988pt{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}}}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}})}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}})}\\[2.84526pt]
