{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}\notin\operatorname{receivers}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\Delta}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)})
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\Delta}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}_{\Delta}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}
, we have
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}\notin\operatorname{receivers}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}_{\Delta}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)})
, as desired;

(a2)

by inductive hypothesis;

(v)

{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}={\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{\rightsquigarrow}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{:}{j}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}\mathtt{m}}_{i}}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}})\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{O}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{O}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\delta_{\text{I}}}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\lambda_{\text{I}}}_{i}}\}\vphantom{x}\!\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}.}}\!{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{i}}}}}\right\}}_{i\in I}}
:

(b1)

since
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\Delta}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mathbin{\!\mathbf{!}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{j}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}_{j}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}}}
with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}_{j}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{j}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{\Delta}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}_{\Delta}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}
, we have
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}_{\Delta}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mathbin{\!\mathbf{!}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{j}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime\prime}_{j}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime\prime}}}}
with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime\prime}_{j}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}_{j}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{j}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime\prime}}
, as desired;

(b2)

by inductive hypothesis.

•

\forall{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}}}}\right)}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}})}}}
, which is the thesis.
∎

Lemma 58
.

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
, then
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}+t\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}+t
.

Proof.

It is straightforward that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}+t}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}=t}\right)}
. We are left
to show that
\forall{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\right)}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}
.

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}
:
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}})}
with

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}
. Then the thesis follows directly from
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}+t}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}+t}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}})}
.

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}
:
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}}
with

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}}
. Then the thesis follows directly from
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}}
.

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}}}
:
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}})}}}
with

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}}
. Then the thesis follows directly from
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}+t}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}+t}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}})}}}
.
∎

Lemma 59
.

Assume that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}!}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{k}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
.
Then, there is
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}
such that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}!}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{k}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
.

Proof.

By association
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}}
,
we have that
\forall{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\right)}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}})}}}
.
Then with the subtyping
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
, it holds that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}\right)}
, and

\forall{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}\right)}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}})}}}
with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}
.

We now apply and invert
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-
\oplus
]
on
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}!}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{k}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
(apply and invert
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-
\mu
]
when necessary) to get

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}\right)}=\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}})}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda_{i}}\}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mathbin{\!\mathbf{!}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{k}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{k}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}}}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda_{k}}\mapsto 0]}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{k}}})}}}]}
,
k\in I
, and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\models{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta_{k}}
.

Since
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
, by inversion of subtyping (
Lemma

40
),

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}\right)}=\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oplus\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}^{\prime}_{j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}_{j}}})}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta^{\prime}_{j}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda^{\prime}_{j}}\}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{j}}\right\}_{j\in J}}
such that
I\subseteq J
, and
\forall i\in I:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{i}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}^{\prime}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{i}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta_{i}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta^{\prime}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda_{i}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda^{\prime}_{i}}
, and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}
. Hence, with
k\in I\subseteq J
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\models{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta_{k}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta^{\prime}_{k}}
, we can apply
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-
\oplus
]
on
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}

to get
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mathbin{\!\mathbf{!}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}^{\prime}_{k}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}_{k}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}}}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda^{\prime}_{k}}\mapsto 0]}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{k}}})}}}]}
.

We are left to show
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
:

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}}\right)}
: directly from
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}}\right)}
;

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mathbin{\!\mathbf{!}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}^{\prime}_{k}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}_{k}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}}}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda^{\prime}_{k}}\mapsto 0]}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{k}}})}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mathbin{\!\mathbf{!}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{k}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{k}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}}}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda_{k}}\mapsto 0]}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{k}}})}}}
: straightforward from
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}^{\prime}_{k}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{k}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{k}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}_{k}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda_{k}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda^{\prime}_{k}}
, and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{k}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{k}}
.

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}]}}\right)}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}]}}\right)}
with
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}\neq{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}
: directly from
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}]}}\right)}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}]}}\right)}
. ∎

Lemma 60
.

Assume that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{k}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
.
Then, there is
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}
such that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{k}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
.

Proof.

By association
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}}
,
we have that
\forall{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\right)}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}})}}}
.
Then with the subtyping
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
, it holds that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}\right)}
, and

\forall{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}\right)}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}})}}}
with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}
.

We now apply and invert
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-
\&
]
on
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{k}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
(apply and invert
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-
\mu
]
when necessary) to get

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mathbin{\!\mathbf{!}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{k}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{k}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}}}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}\right)}=\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{i}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}_{i}}})}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda_{i}}\}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}\right\}_{i\in I}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}})}}}]}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}]}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda_{k}}\mapsto 0]}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{k}}})}}}]}
,
k\in I
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\models{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda_{k}}
, and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{k}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}_{k}}
.

Since
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
, by inversion of subtyping (
Lemma

40
),

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{unf}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}\right)}=\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\&\!\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}^{\prime}_{j}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime\prime}_{j}}})}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta^{\prime}_{j}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda^{\prime}_{j}}\}}\mathbin{\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}.}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{j}}\right\}_{j\in J}}
such that
J\subseteq I
, and
\forall i\in j:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{i}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}^{\prime}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime\prime}_{i}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta_{i}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta^{\prime}_{i}},{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda_{i}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda^{\prime}_{i}}
, and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{i}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{i}}
. By
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}
,
we know that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mathbin{\!\mathbf{!}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime\prime\prime}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}}}
with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{k}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S_{k}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime\prime\prime}}
, and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}}
. Hence, by applying
Lemma

52
, we have
k\in J
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}_{k}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}^{\prime}_{k}}
, and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime\prime\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime\prime}_{k}}
.
Then along with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}\models{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta_{k}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta^{\prime}_{k}}
, we can apply
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-
\&
]
on
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}

to get
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}})}}}]}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}]}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda^{\prime}_{k}}\mapsto 0]}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{k}}})}}}]}
.

We are left to show
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
:

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}}\right)}
: directly from
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}}\right)}
;

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}})}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathcal{M}^{\prime}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{;\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}})}}}
: straightforward from

Lemma 61
.

Assume that
\forall{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}:\exists\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}}\rangle:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}}\rangle}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}
.
Then, there is
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}
such that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\alpha}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}

Apply
Lemma

(and
Lemma

when necessary).

Lemma 62
.

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
and
\operatorname{end}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}})
, then
\operatorname{end}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}})
.

Proof.

Straightforward from
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
,
[T-Sub]
, and
[T-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}
]
.
∎

Lemma 63

(Narrowing)
.

If

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
, then

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}
.

Proof.

By induction on the derivation of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}
. Most
cases hold by inverting the typing
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}
, inserting (possibly vacuous) instances of rule
[T-Sub]
,
and then applying the induction hypothesis.
We develop some interesting cases.

•

Case
[T-Sub]
: by the transitivity of subtyping (
Lemma

32
).

•

Case
[T-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}
]
:
by applying
[T-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}
]
on
\operatorname{end}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}})
(from
\operatorname{end}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}})
and
Lemma

62
).

•

Case
[T-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}
-Call]
: by
Lemma

62
and induction hypothesis.

•

Case
[T-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}
]
: by
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}
and induction hypothesis.

•

Case
[T-Kill]
: by
Lemma

62
and induction hypothesis.

•

Case
[T-Failed]
: by the fact that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{timeout}}}[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}]
can be typed by any typing environment.

•

Case
[T-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}
]
: by
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\oslash}
and
Lemma

62
.

•

Case
[T-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sigma}
]
:
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{1}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{2}}
such that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{1}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{3}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mathbin{\!\mathbf{!}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{3}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}}}]}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{3}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sigma}}}}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}\models{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta}
, and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{2}}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s^{\prime}}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}]}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}}}
. Since
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
, we have
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}_{1}}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}_{2}}
such that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}_{1}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}_{3}}{}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}\mapsto{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}\mathbin{\!\mathbf{!}\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}}})}}\mathbin{\!\cdot\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}_{3}}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\right)}}}]}
with

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S}=({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}S^{\prime}}=({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta}},{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}})
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}_{3}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{3}}
, and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}_{2}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{2}}
. By induction hypothesis, we obtain
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}_{3}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sigma}}}}}
. We are left to show that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}_{2}}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s^{\prime}}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}]}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}})}}}
, which follows from
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{2}}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s^{\prime}}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{r}}}}}]}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T^{\prime}}
,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}_{2}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\leqslant}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma_{2}}
, and
[T-Sub]
.
∎

Lemma 64

(Substitution)
.

Assume
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}\vdash{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}}}
with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
defined.
Then
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\nicefrac{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}}}\right\}}}}}
.

Proof.

By induction on the derivation of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathpunct{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}x}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}T}})}}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}
.
∎

Lemma 65

(Subject Congruence)
.

Assume

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
. Then,
\exists{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
such that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\equiv}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
and

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}}}
.

Proof.

The proof is similar to that of Theorem 3.11 in
[
32
]
, and in all corresponding cases, we have
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
.
We have three additional cases regarding delay and queue congruence rules.

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{delay}}}({0})}{\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
:

(
\Rightarrow
)

suppose
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{delay}}}({0})}{\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}}}}
. By
[T-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}
]
, we have

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}0}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}}
, and hence,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}}
, as desired.

(
\Leftarrow
)

suppose
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}}
, which follows by

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}0}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}}
. Hence, by inversion of
[T-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}
]
, we have

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{delay}}}({0})}{\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}}}}
, as desired.

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}\mathbin{\!:\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}}}}\right){({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}_{1}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}\cdots\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}_{n}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\epsilon}}})}}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
: we must have
\operatorname{end}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}})
and
\operatorname{end}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}})
, and hence,
we conclude with
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
by typing rule
[T-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}
]
.

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\equiv{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
holds by the order-swapping congruence on queues: we apply the same reordering on the queue types of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
, getting a congruent typing environment
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
that satisfies the statement.
∎

Appendix D
Proofs for Subject Reduction and Type Safety

Lemma 66
.

If
\operatorname{end}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}})
, then

\operatorname{end}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}})
.

Proof.

From
\operatorname{end}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}})
, we have that

\forall{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\right)}:{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}})}
, which follows directly that

\forall{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}\right)}(={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{dom}\!\left({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\right)}):{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}\!\left({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}\right)}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}{\mathbin{\mathbf{,\,}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathbf{end}}})}
. Then the thesis holds
by applying
[T-Sub]
and

[T-
\operatorname{end}
]
.
∎

Lemma 67
.

If
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P})}
is defined, then

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P})}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}})}
.

Proof.

By induction on the syntactic constructs of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
.
The case of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{delay}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta}})}{\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}}}

is excluded due to the undefined nature of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P})}
.

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}
:
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P})}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{0}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
. The thesis holds trivially.

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\boldsymbol{\mathsf{cerr}}}
:
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P})}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\boldsymbol{\mathsf{cerr}}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
. The thesis holds trivially.

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{1}}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{2}}
:
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P})}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{1}})}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mid}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{2}})}
. By the induction hypothesis, we have
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{1}})}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{1}}})}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{2}})}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{2}}})}
. Hence,

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{1}})}})}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{2}})}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{1}}})}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q_{2}}})}
, which is the thesis.

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}\right){{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}}
:
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P})}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left(\mathbf{\nu}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}\right){{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q})}}}
.
By the induction hypothesis, we have
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q})}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}})}
. Hence,

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}})}\setminus(\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}_{i}}}]}\right\}}}_{i\in I}\cup\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}_{i}}}]}}^{\mathscr{Q}}\right\}}}_{i\in I})
=
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q})}})}\setminus(\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}_{i}}}]}\right\}}}_{i\in I}\cup\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}_{i}}}]}}^{\mathscr{Q}}\right\}}}_{i\in I})
, which is the thesis.

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{def}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}(\widetilde{x})={{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}}}\;\mathbf{in}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}}}}
:
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P})}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbf{def}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}(\widetilde{x})={{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}}}\;\mathbf{in}\;{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}})}}}
. By the induction hypothesis,
we have
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}})}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}}})}
.
Hence,
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}}})}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}})}\setminus\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{\widetilde{x}\right\}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}})}})}\cup{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}})}\setminus\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{\widetilde{x}\right\}}}
with

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}}\!\left\langle{\widetilde{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}}\right\rangle}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\nicefrac{{\widetilde{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}}}{{\widetilde{x}}}}\right\}}}})}
, which is the thesis.

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}^{\mathfrak{n}}[\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}]\mathbin{\!\oplus\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{m}}}}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}d}}\rangle\vphantom{x}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}}}
:
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P})}})}=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\right\}}}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}})}
if
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}\leq\mathfrak{n}
, and
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P})}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{timeout}}}[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}]})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}})}=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\right\}}}
if
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}>\mathfrak{n}
, as desired.

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}^{\mathfrak{n}}[\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}]}\mathbin{\!\sum_{i\in I}\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\mathtt{m}}_{\mathnormal{i}}}}}({x_{i}})\vphantom{x}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{i}}}}}{}
: similarly to the case
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}}^{\mathfrak{n}}[\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}}}]\mathbin{\!\oplus\!}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{m}}}}\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}d}}\rangle\vphantom{x}\mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}}}
.

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{cancel}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}})}{\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}}
:
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P})}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{cancel}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}})}{\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q})}}}
. The thesis holds as
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}})}=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\right\}}}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P})}})}=\mathord{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\left\{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}c}\right\}}}
.

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{delay}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t^{\prime}}})}{\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}}
:
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P})}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{delay}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t^{\prime}}-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}})}{\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}}
. The thesis holds straightforward from
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}})}
and
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P})}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}})}
.

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{try}}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}\ \operatorname{\text{{catch}}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}}}}
:
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P})}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{try}}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q})}}\ \operatorname{\text{{catch}}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q^{\prime}})}}}
. The thesis holds straightforward from
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}})}
,
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P})}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q})}})}
, and the induction hypothesis

{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q})}})}={\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\operatorname{subjP}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}})}
.

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\boldsymbol{\mathtt{timeout}}}[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}]
: the thesis holds trivially by
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P})}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
.

•

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}}\mathrel{\!\blacktriangleright\!}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sigma}}}
: the thesis holds directly from
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\Psi_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}({\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P})}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
.
∎

Proof.

(24)

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\!\to\!{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}

(25)

By
[R-Ins]
and

[R-TC]
, we know that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\to{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}

is derived from either
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\rightarrowtail{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
or
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\rightharpoonup{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
. Therefore,
the proof can proceed by induction on the derivation of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\rightarrowtail{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}

and
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\rightharpoonup{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
.
When the reduction holds by rule
[R-Ctx]
, a further structural induction on the reduction context
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{C}}
is required.
When the reduction involves a try-catch context
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{E}}
, we can observe from the typing rule

[T-Try]
that
{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{E}}}\!\left[{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}\right]
is typed using the same typing environment
as
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
. This implies that we only need to focus on reductions where
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{E}}=[\,]
.
Most cases hold by inversion of the typing
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Theta}}\cdot{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\vdash}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}}}
, and by applying the induction hypothesis.

See
22

Proof.

From the hypothesis
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\mathrel{\to{}^{\!\!\!*}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
,
we know that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{0}}\to{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{1}}\to\cdots\to{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{n}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
(for some
n
).
The proof proceeds by induction on
n
.
The base case
n=0
is immediate: we have
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
,
hence
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
is well-typed – and since the term
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\boldsymbol{\mathsf{cerr}}}
is not typeable,
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
cannot contain such a term.
In the inductive case
n=m+1
, we know (by the induction hypothesis) that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{m}}
is well-typed,
and we apply
Thm.

21
to conclude that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{m+1}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
is also well-typed and has no
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\boldsymbol{\mathsf{cerr}}}
subterms.
∎

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P_{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}}
playing role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}
in session
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
is a branching on

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}]}
from
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}
(possibly within a process definition).
Therefore, by
[R-In]
in
Fig.

3
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}
can correspondingly reduce to
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
by receiving
an endpoint
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s^{\prime}}}[{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}^{\prime}}}]}

from the message queue of
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}
in session
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}
(possibly after a finite number of transitions under rule
[R-
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}X}
]
).
The resulting continuation process
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}

is typed by
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
.
The assertion that there exists

\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle
such that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}}

follows from

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9},}}{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{q}}}}}{:}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\mathtt{m}}}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
(apply
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-,
\tau
]
when necessary),

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}}
,
and
Thm.

14
.

: by

, it holds that

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}={\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+}\,{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}
.

in

.

–

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P}\neq{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\operatorname{\text{{delay}}}({{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\delta}})}{\ \mathbin{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\!.\!}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}Q}}}
: directly from
Lemma

20
.

The assertion that there exists

\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle
such that
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V^{\prime}}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G^{\prime}}}\rangle}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}}}

follows from

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\xrightarrow{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}t}}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma^{\prime}}
,

{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\langle{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathbb{V}}};{{\color[rgb]{0.43,0.21,0.1}G}}\rangle}\mathrel{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\sqsubseteq}_{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}s}}}{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}}}
,

.

•

Case
[
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.9}\Gamma}
-
\mu
]
: by induction hypothesis.

Regarding the preservation of
{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}P^{\prime}}
w.r.t.

, we need to address the following additional cases when

, or

•

only plays role
{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.5,0,0}\mathtt{p}}}}
